Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a triple *Masters Edition IV* draft from the Top 8 of an online Premier Event! *Masters Edition IV* is on sale on **[Magic Online](https://web.archive.org/web/20140527070414/http://www.wizards.com/Magic/Digital/MagicOnline.aspx)**.

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da648_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20110216000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=2022631)

### Champion

**Matteso, 2-0**
